Second transcription's note: Step 2 q3/q4 and Step 4 q4/q5: on a joint return the dependent can be claimed; otherwise you must not be claimable as someone else's dependent (sa.dependent, which also covers your spouse). Married persons, tie-breaker, divorced-parent and multiple-support situations are refused.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 21, Multiple support agreements: "If no one person contributed over half of the support of your relative (or a person who lived with you all year as a member of your household) but you and another person(s) provided more than half of your relative's support, special rules may apply"; i1040gi p. 19, Step 4: "Any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law". The reply now requires dependent.lawful and 12 months for sibling_descendant, the reading of finding L1; the project, after the L1 fix, does the same (executed: /tmp/adj-s19r3/w_sibdesc_false.json, the round-2 witness with lawful no, gives dependent.claimed no, odc_amount $0.00, f1040 line 19 $500.00; with lawful yes claimed yes, $500.00, $1,000.00), so no value differs on that point. Its household_member route now also tests 12 months (last round's difference (a) is gone). Remaining differences: (b) it refuses dependent.multiple_support on every entry, including a qualifying child, whose dependency has no support test of the filer (classm.s3.4 and classm.s3.4.deepseek dep_a, a child aged 2 with multiple_support yes, completed: project yes, candidate refuses; classm.tcw.3, generated 126, 186, 243 on refused returns). The rise in completed-return candidate-refuses (2 last round, 83 now) is the bank, not the reply: the reply refused dependent.multiple_support on every entry last round too, and the regenerated bank (1,323 fixtures, partial bases every fourth reached fixture) now draws partial records from returns carrying the classm.s3.4 child (inferred from the witnesses; triage records only six per kind). Its household_member route now asks dependent.lawful as well (candidate-unknown on fixtures that predate the question, for example classm.clcw26.2.deepseek dep_b and classm.f8880.2.deepseek dep_a, completed): an extra question with no amount, because the project's household_member answer already states the condition ("if your relationship did not violate local law", dependent.relationship). (c) Its joint-return exception to sa.dependent (mfj) differs only on returns the project refuses at sa.standard (classm.sc.2 dep_b, the one value): scope on that kind. The project-refuses are dependent.claimed_no_tin, the cited screen of i1040gi p. 22 ("You must enter each dependent's social security number (SSN)") (classm.eic.8.deepseek, classm.carloan.1, classm.f8959.2, classm.ltc.2, classm.s1.2) and dependent.income_exception (pp. 19-20; classm.sc.1), all on refused returns: scope. The project-unknown are partials withholding tin, which the interview asks of every listed person. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/dependent.json; 1,323 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,268 partial records): 6,442 of 6,899 comparisons agree; 127 candidate-refuses, 157 candidate-unknown, 103 project-refuses, 69 project-unknown, 1 value; on returns the full interview completes: 83 candidate-refuses, 22 candidate-unknown.

Second transcription's note: Excluded categories (divorced-parent rule, multiple support agreement, married qualifying child) are refused rather than computed; a dependent who is a relative only by living with you all year does not count. Paying over half the cost of the home is tested at return level.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 14, Test 2: "in which one of the following also lived for more than half of the year (if half or less, see Exception to time lived with you, later)"; p501 p. 9, Death or birth. As last round, the reply tests months of 7 or more with no part-year route (born_died, placed, part_year_home were published), so a parent or child who lived with you more than half of the part of the year they were alive or placed is dropped: stage15.f2441.parent_died_part_year, stage8.hoh.parent.died_lived_part_year and stage8.hoh.adopted_part_year (completed; project yes, candidate no). The reply no longer refuses multiple_support here; its married, other_claimant and divorced_rule refusals are the project's (candidate-unknown only on partials withholding them, for example classm.qdcg.1 and classm.sc.4). Its "dependent only because of living with you all year" exclusion (claimed and a listed relative) is the project's. The project-unknown are fixtures without dependent.born_died, which the interview asks for a relative with 6 months or fewer (classm.f8962.5.deepseek dep_b, classm.f1040.2.deepseek dep_c, completed), where the candidate does not need it because it has no part-year rule. The project-refuses are dependent.claimed_no_tin, the cited screen of i1040gi p. 22 ("You must enter each dependent's social security number (SSN)") (classm.eic.8.deepseek, classm.carloan.1, classm.f8959.2, classm.ltc.2) and the multiple support screen at dependent.qualifying_relative (i1040gi p. 21, Multiple support agreements; classm.asset.1, classm.gift.1), all on refused returns: scope. Second transcription's note: Read 'your parent' as father or mother only (relationship parent), not a grandparent.

Ruling: scope; i1040gi p. 14, Test 1: "You paid over half the cost of keeping up a home that was the main home for all of 2025 of your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, except under a multiple support agreement"; p501 p. 10, Table 4, note 4: "If you can claim a person as a dependent only because of a multiple support agreement, that person isn't a qualifying person." For relationship parent both lines apply the project's dependency tests and parent_home_paid; the reply adds "not multiple_support", which the project reaches as a refusal (the multiple support screen at dependent.qualifying_relative, i1040gi p. 21; classm.asset.1, classm.qdcg.3). No value and no candidate refusal or unknown differs this round. The other project-refuses are dependent.claimed_no_tin, the cited screen of i1040gi p. 22 ("You must enter each dependent's social security number (SSN)") (classm.eic.8.deepseek, classm.carloan.1, classm.f8959.2, classm.ltc.2), all on refused returns; the project-unknown are partials withholding tin (classm.f2441.1.deepseek, classm.f2441.5.deepseek, classm.f8889.4.deepseek), which the interview asks of every listed person. The project-unknown are fixtures without dependent.ptc_files (classm.f8960.2.deepseek, classm.sd.2.deepseek, classm.clcw.2.deepseek), which the interview asks only for a claimed dependent on a return with a Form 1095-A. The candidate refusals inherit its dependent.claimed (model error there). amb.2025.ptc_dependent_filing in both. Second transcription's note: For the lifetime learning credit only expenses required to be paid to the institution count; the aqee worksheet's ws4_llc (institution expenses less adjustments, floored at zero) is used.

Ruling: model error; i8863 p. 3, Who cannot claim a credit: "5. The student has not been issued a TIN by the due date of his or her 2025 return (including extensions)", and Student qualifications: "5. Filers and students must have been issued a TIN by the due date of their 2025 return (including extensions)." i8863 p. 2: "You may be able to claim an education credit if you, your spouse, or a dependent you claim on your tax return was a student enrolled at or attending an eligible educational institution." As last round the candidate omits f8863.paid and the filers' TIN test (household.all_tin, amb.2025.edu_filer_tin, owner decision D7(a)); it now reads the project's aqee definition for the arithmetic and its own dependent.claimed (multiple support refused for a child). Witness: generated 99 (the taxpayer has no TIN; the round-2 generated 47, renumbered by the generator change): the project sends dep_a and dep_b to line 31 ($12,706.94 and $5,509.61) and hides lines 27 to 30; the candidate keeps them on lines 27 to 30 and shows $0.00 here (the two values), against the quoted item 5. The amount (institution-paid expenses less lines 2a to 2c, floored at zero) is the project's aqee ws4_llc. The candidate-unknown records are returns with f8863.paid no, where the interview never asks the dependent's education answers (classm.f2441.1.deepseek to .5: the candidate needs dependent.edu); its refusals are its own edu_over gate on stage19.refuse.period_over_dependent, which the project also refuses, and its multiple_support placement (classm.s3.4.deepseek without dependent.edu, completed). Its edu_over gate omits the project's "edu_adj above zero", which a Yes to the reworded question implies (assistance above a period's expenses is above zero). The project-refuses are its cited screens on refused returns: dependent.claimed_no_tin (generated 111, 250, 285; i1040gi p. 22), dependent.married (generated 23; i1040gi p. 21), dependent.other_claimant (generated 75) and dependent.divorced_rule (generated 210): scope. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/dependent.json; 1,323 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 2,268 partial records): 3,167 of 6,899 comparisons agree; 1 candidate-refuses, 3,719 candidate-unknown, 10 project-refuses, 2 value; on returns the full interview completes: 1 candidate-refuses, 1,035 candidate-unknown.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: dependent.one
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One (this person)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}

  - id: dependent.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Relationship test (Step 1): son, daughter, stepchild, foster child, brother, sister, stepbrother, stepsister, half brother, half sister, or a descendant of any of them"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_age_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Age test (Step 1): under age 19 and younger than you, or under age 24, a full-time student and younger than you, or any age and permanently and totally disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: qc.student_age_limit}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.student, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.younger, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.disabled, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1: meets the conditions to be your qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.self_support, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "Lived with you for more than half of 2025 is read from the months input (7 or more), which already applies the temporary-absence, birth/death and adoption/placement rules."

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qr_close
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 relatives who need not live with you: child or descendant, brother/sister/half or step sibling or their son or daughter, father, mother or an ancestor or sibling of either, step-parent or in-law"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "child"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent_relative"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "in_law"}]}

  - id: dependent.h_rel_qr_household
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: any other person (other than your spouse) who lived with you all year as a member of your household if your relationship didn't violate local law"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "sibling_descendant"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "household_member"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "other"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.months, {const: 12, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.lawful, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_qr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4: meets the conditions to be your qualifying relative"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr_close, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr_household, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.gross_income, {param: qr.gross_income_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.supported, {const: true}]}
    note: "Not being a qualifying child of another taxpayer is screened by dependent.other_claimant (refused on dependent.claimed). The sheltered-workshop exception to the gross income test is not modeled; gross income is taken as entered."

  - id: dependent.claimed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You can claim this person as a dependent (Steps 2 and 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qr, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.canada_mexico, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    refuses: [dependent.other_claimant, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married, dependent.multiple_support]
    note: "Step 2 q3/q4 and Step 4 q4/q5: on a joint return the dependent can be claimed; otherwise you must not be claimable as someone else's dependent (sa.dependent, which also covers your spouse). Married persons, tie-breaker, divorced-parent and multiple-support situations are refused."

  - id: dependent.ctc_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3: you can claim the child tax credit for this person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: ctc.age_limit}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.work_ssn, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.ctc_ssn, {const: true}]}
    note: "household.ctc_ssn is read as 'you, or your spouse if filing jointly, have an SSN valid for employment' (Step 3 q4)."

  - id: dependent.odc_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 3 and 5: you can claim the credit for other dependents for this person"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.citizen, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.all_tin, {const: true}]}
    note: "household.all_tin is read as 'you, and your spouse if filing jointly, have an SSN or ITIN issued by the due date' (Step 5 q1 and Before you begin)."

  - id: dependent.h_ctc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Child tax credit amount per qualifying child ($2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ctc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.ctc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 5 share for this person (line 4 count x $2,200)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_ctc_per
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero

  - id: dependent.h_odc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit for other dependents amount per dependent ($500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: odc.per_dependent}

  - id: dependent.odc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 7 share for this person (line 6 count x $500)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_odc_per
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.odc_person, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero

  - id: dependent.h_actc_per
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional child tax credit amount per qualifying child ($1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: actc.per_child}

  - id: dependent.actc_amount
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Schedule 8812 line 16b share for this person (qualifying children x $1,700)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_actc_per
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.ctc_child, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero

  - id: dependent.hoh_test1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 1: you paid over half the cost of keeping up a home that was the main home for all of 2025 of your parent whom you can claim as a dependent, except under a multiple support agreement"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.relationship, {const: "parent"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.parent_home_paid, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.multiple_support, {const: false}]}
    note: "Read 'your parent' as father or mother only (relationship parent), not a grandparent."

  - id: dependent.hoh_test2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household Test 2: this person lived in your home for more than half the year and is your dependent (not only because of living with you all year) or your unmarried qualifying child who isn't your dependent"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qr_close, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.other_claimant, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married]
    note: "Excluded categories (divorced-parent rule, multiple support agreement, married qualifying child) are refused rather than computed; a dependent who is a relative only by living with you all year does not count. Paying over half the cost of the home is tested at return level."

  - id: dependent.eic_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Step 3: qualifying child for the EIC (relationship, age, not filing a joint return, lived with you in the United States for more than half of 2025)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_rel_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_age_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.joint_return, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.us_half, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [dependent.other_claimant, dependent.married, dependent.divorced_rule]

  - id: dependent.eic_ssn_child
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC qualifying child who has a valid SSN for the EIC"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.dc_qp
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 2441 qualifying person: a qualifying child under age 13 whom you can claim as a dependent, or a disabled dependent who wasn't able to care for themselves and lived with you for more than half the year"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_qc, {const: true}]}
                - op: test
                  any:
                    - {op: test, lt: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                    - op: test
                      all:
                        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_under13, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, ge: [dependent.months, {const: 7, type: Count}]}
    note: "Persons who would be dependents but for gross income, a joint return or your being claimable are handled by the return-level f2441.nondependent screen, not here."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2c
    printed: "2(c)"
    label: "Check here if the qualifying person was over age 12 and was disabled"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_incapable, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, ge: [dependent.age, {const: 14, type: Count}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.age, {param: dc.child_age}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_over12_care, {const: true}]}
    note: "A person age 14 or more at the end of 2025 was over 12 all year; for a person who turned 13 in 2025 the box depends on whether included care was provided after the 13th birthday (dc_over12_care)."

  - id: dependent.dc_line2d
    printed: "2(d)"
    label: "Qualified expenses you incurred and paid in 2025 for the person listed in column (a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.dc_paid
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.dc_qp, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    note: "The benefits shown on line 28 are subtracted on Form 2441 (per the dc_paid input), not here."

  - id: dependent.dc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Count of this person as a Form 2441 qualifying person"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: param, ref: dependent.one}
    note: "A choose cannot return a Count, so this is 1 per entry; the Form 2441 total must sum it with where dependent.dc_qp is true."

  - id: dependent.ptc_magi_counted
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8962 Worksheet 1-2: modified AGI of this dependent if required to file an income tax return because their income meets the filing threshold"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.ptc_magi
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [dependent.ptc_files, {const: true}]}
          then: dependent.h_zero

  - id: dependent.h_edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet line 3: total adjustments (lines 2a, 2b and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [dependent.edu_a2025, dependent.edu_a2026, dependent.edu_refunds]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_over_gate
    printed: "(gate)"
    label: "Assistance and refunds exceed an academic period's expenses (per-period allocation not supported)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_over, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_aoc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student qualifies for the American opportunity credit (Part III lines 23-26, Form 1098-T, TIN and EIN)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line23, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line24, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line25, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_line26, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_ein, {const: true}]}

  - id: dependent.h_edu_llc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Student's expenses go to line 31 (lifetime learning credit)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.claimed, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.tin, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.edu_1098t, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: false}]}
    note: "A missing Form 1098-T (without an exception) or student TIN is read as no education credit for this student, giving zero rather than a refusal."

  - id: dependent.edu_line27
    printed: "27"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Don't enter more than $4,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line27}
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_gate]

  - id: dependent.edu_line28
    printed: "28"
    label: "Subtract $2,000 from line 27. If zero or less, enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line28}
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_gate]

  - id: dependent.edu_line29
    printed: "29"
    label: "Multiply line 28 by 25% (0.25)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line29}
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_gate]

  - id: dependent.edu_line30
    printed: "30"
    label: "If line 28 is zero, enter the amount from line 27. Otherwise, add $2,000 to the amount on line 29 and enter the result. Skip line 31"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: line30}
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_aoc, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_gate]

  - id: dependent.edu_line31
    printed: "31"
    label: "Adjusted qualified education expenses (see instructions). Include the total of all amounts from all Parts III, line 31, on Part II, line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}
          then: {def: aqee, bind: {inst: dependent.edu_inst, mat: dependent.edu_mat, adj: dependent.h_edu_adj}, line: ws4_llc}
        - guard: {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [dependent.h_edu_llc, {const: true}]}}
          then: dependent.h_zero
    refuses: [dependent.h_edu_over_gate]
    note: "For the lifetime learning credit only expenses required to be paid to the institution count; the aqee worksheet's ws4_llc (institution expenses less adjustments, floored at zero) is used."
```
```
